### Task 7: Search assembly — `FusionExec`, `DocFetchExec`, `TailMergeExec`, the planner, paging, groups, get, count and scroll

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/exec/{fusion.rs,doc_fetch.rs,tail_merge.rs,planner.rs,groups.rs,scroll.rs,get.rs,project.rs}`, `crates/operon-query/src/text/pkdict.rs`, `crates/operon-query/tests/search.rs`.

**Consumes:**
- Tasks 5–6 operators and `EffectiveSort`. Task 4: `ReadView`.
- M1.1: `CollectionSnapshot::get_by_pk`, `extract`, `coerce`, `IndexValue`, `SOURCE_COLUMN`, `INGEST_PARTITION_COLUMN`, `INGEST_OFFSET_COLUMN`, `vector_column`.
- Lance: `Dataset::take_rows(&[u64], ProjectionRequest::from_columns(cols, dataset.schema()))`.

**Produces:**

```rust
// exec/fusion.rs
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct FusionExec { /* inputs: Vec<Arc<dyn ExecutionPlan>>, fusion: Fusion, k: usize */ }
impl FusionExec { pub fn new(inputs: Vec<Arc<dyn ExecutionPlan>>, fusion: Fusion, k: usize) -> Self; }
impl ExecutionPlan for FusionExec {}                               // output: ranked_schema, (fused score desc, pk asc), ≤ k rows
pub fn fuse(lists: &[Vec<Ranked>], fusion: &Fusion) -> Vec<Ranked>; // the pure function the operator applies (tests)

// exec/doc_fetch.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)] pub struct FetchColumns { pub source: bool, pub vectors: Vec<usize> /* indexes into schema.vectors */,
                                                                 pub sparse: Vec<usize> /* indexes into schema.sparse_vectors */ }
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct DocFetchExec { /* input, view, columns */ }
impl DocFetchExec { pub fn new(input: Arc<dyn ExecutionPlan>, view: Arc<ReadView>, columns: FetchColumns) -> Self; }
impl ExecutionPlan for DocFetchExec {}  // output: ranked_schema + _source Utf8 (nullable), _vectors Binary (postcard BTreeMap<String, Vec<f32>>),
                                        // _sparse Binary (postcard BTreeMap<String, SparseVector>), _seq_no UInt64, _partition UInt32
pub async fn fetch_rows(view: &ReadView, row_ids: &[u64], columns: &FetchColumns) -> Result<Vec<FetchedRow>, ServiceError>;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] pub struct FetchedRow { pub row_id: u64, pub pk: PrimaryKey, pub source: Option<Map<String, Value>>,
                                                           pub vectors: BTreeMap<String, Vec<f32>>, pub sparse_vectors: BTreeMap<String, SparseVector>,
                                                           pub seq_no: u64, pub partition: u32 }

// exec/tail_merge.rs
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct TailMergeExec { /* durable: PK-ordered input, tail snapshot, filter rows */ }
impl TailMergeExec { pub fn new(durable: Arc<dyn ExecutionPlan>, view: Arc<ReadView>, tail_rows: RowSet, after: Option<PrimaryKey>, limit: Option<usize>) -> Self; }
impl ExecutionPlan for TailMergeExec {}  // input and output: _rowid UInt64, _pk Binary, in PK order; shadowed durable rows dropped, tail entries interleaved

// text/pkdict.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug)] pub struct PkDictCache { /* moka weighted by bytes: split ulid → Arc<tantivy::Index> with warm _pk dictionary, postings and _rowid */ }
impl PkDictCache { pub fn new(max_bytes: u64 /* 256 MiB */) -> Self;
                   pub async fn cursor(&self, view: &ReadView, split: usize, after: Option<&[u8]>) -> Result<PkCursor, ServiceError>; }
pub struct PkCursor; impl PkCursor { pub fn next(&mut self) -> Result<Option<(Vec<u8> /* canonical pk */, u64 /* row id */)>, ServiceError>; }  // live, unshadowed only

// exec/planner.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] pub struct SearchConfig { pub limits: SearchLimits, pub parallelism: usize /* 8 */,
    pub scroll_sort_threshold: u64 /* 100_000 */, pub pk_dict_cache_bytes: u64 /* 256 MiB */, pub stats_cache_entries: u64 /* 1_000_000 */,
    pub agg_memory_limit: u64 /* 500_000_000, Tantivy's default */, pub agg_bucket_limit: u32 /* 65_000, Tantivy's default */ }
pub struct SearchPlanner { /* config, ann config, caches */ }
impl SearchPlanner {
    pub fn new(config: SearchConfig, ann: AnnConfig) -> Self;
    pub async fn search(&self, view: Arc<ReadView>, request: SearchRequest) -> Result<SearchResponse, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn get(&self, view: &ReadView, pks: &[PrimaryKey], select: &Projection) -> Result<Vec<Option<StoredDoc>>, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn count(&self, view: Arc<ReadView>, filter: Option<Query>) -> Result<u64, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn scroll(&self, view: Arc<ReadView>, filter: Option<Query>, after: Option<PrimaryKey>, limit: usize, select: &Projection)
        -> Result<(Vec<StoredDoc>, Option<PrimaryKey>), ServiceError>;
}
// exec/project.rs
pub fn filter_source(source: &Map<String, Value>, filter: &SourceFilter) -> Option<Map<String, Value>>;
pub fn field_values(schema: &CollectionSchema, source: &Map<String, Value>, fields: &[String]) -> BTreeMap<String, Vec<FieldValue>>;
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Planning a search** (`SearchPlanner::search`):
   1. `validate_request`; `EffectiveSort::of`.
   2. Build `R(r)` for each top-level retriever:
      - `Vector` → `AnnExec` with `allow = FilterBitmapExec(request.filter ∧ r.filter)`, or none when both are absent;
      - `Text` → `TantivySearchExec(query, request.filter, k)`;
      - `Fused` → `FusionExec` of its inputs' plans;
      - `Rescore` → the input's plan, then rule 3;
      - `Sparse` → `SparseExec` with `allow = FilterBitmapExec(request.filter ∧ r.filter)` (or none) and `corpus = FilterBitmapExec(r.params.idf_corpus)` (or none).
   3. Candidates:
      - score mode with one retriever and no fusion → that plan;
      - several retrievers, or a present `fusion` → `FusionExec(plans, fusion, window)` with `window = max over retrievers of k`;
      - field mode → one `TantivySearchExec` in field mode (the Text retriever's query, or `MatchAll`) with `k = offset + limit` and `search_after`;
      - no retrievers in score mode is refused by validation.
   4. Execute with `datafusion::physical_plan::collect(plan, SessionContext::new().task_ctx())`, then convert to `Ranked`.
   5. Score mode: keep `score >= score_threshold` when set, then `search_after`, then sort by the effective order. The `_sort` column carries the field values of field mode.
   6. `groups` (rule 5) replace `hits` when `group_by` is set. Otherwise skip `offset` and take `limit`.
   7. Fetch the page: `DocFetchExec` over the page's rows with `FetchColumns { source: select.source != None || !select.fields.is_empty() || highlight.is_some(), vectors: the select.vectors names that are dense vectors, sparse: those that are sparse vectors }` (overview A30). A name that is neither is `InvalidArgument`.
   8. Hits:
      - `score`, and `sort_values = EffectiveSort::sort_values`;
      - `source = filter_source(source, &select.source)`;
      - `vectors` and `sparse_vectors` = exactly the requested dense and sparse names the doc has (S10);
      - `fields = field_values(...)`;
      - `highlight` from Task 8.
   9. Totals and aggregations: `total` (rule 4); `aggregations` (Task 8); `read_token = view.read_token`; `hot_used = view.hot_used.kinds()`.
2. **Fusion** (`fuse`, Rulings 4–5). Each input list is already in (score desc, pk asc) order, and its rank is its 1-based position. Documents are keyed by row id, and fused scores are summed in input order:
   - `Rrf { k }`: `s(d) = Σ 1.0f32 / (k as f32 + rank as f32)`.
   - `Dbsf`: per list, Qdrant's `distr_norm` in f32:
     - a list of 1 → 0.5;
     - else Welford (`mean += δ/k; agg += δ_old·δ_new`), `var = agg / (n − 1)`, `σ = √var`, `lo = mean − 3σ`, `hi = mean + 3σ`;
     - `lo == hi` → 0.5; else `(s − lo) / (hi − lo)`, **not clamped**.

     Then sum.
   - `WeightedSum { weights }`: `Σ wᵢ · sᵢ` over the lists containing *d*.

   The fused list is sorted by (fused score desc, pk asc) and truncated to k.
3. **`Rescore { input, field, query, k }`:** take the input's candidates (at most its k) and fetch `_vector_i` with one `fetch_rows`. Candidates without the vector are dropped. Score with `vector::score` (S9) and keep the best k by (score desc, pk asc).
4. **Totals:**
   - `None` → `total: None`.
   - `Exact`:
     - with only a `Text` retriever, or field mode → `TantivySearchExec::count` (every match of `Text ∧ filter`);
     - with no retrievers → the filter's match count;
     - otherwise → the number of distinct candidates after `score_threshold`.

     Relation `Eq`.
   - `UpTo(n)`: the same count c; `c <= n` → `{c, Eq}`, else `{n, Gte}`.
5. **`group_by { field, group_size, limit }`:**
   1. Walk the candidates in order and fetch their sources in pages of 256.
   2. The group keys of a hit are its values at the field's `source_path` (or the JSON path), through `extract` and `coerce` for typed fields, and as the JSON scalar for JSON paths (strings, integers and bools only). A hit joins every group of its keys.
   3. Groups are ordered by their first hit. A group keeps at most `group_size` hits, and collection stops once `limit` groups are full or the candidates run out.
   4. `HitGroup.key` is the key as a `FieldValue`.
6. **Scroll** (Ruling 15), `after` exclusive and PK order (S8):
   1. The durable side:
      - no filter, or a filter with `|A_d| > scroll_sort_threshold`: a k-way merge over every split's `PkCursor` (live and unshadowed only; checked against `A_d` when a filter is present);
      - otherwise: the pks of `A_d` read from `_pk` fast fields, sorted, and cut at `after`.
   2. The tail side: `tail.keys_after(after, ∞)`, with `Present` docs kept when their row id is in the filter's tail part, and `Deleted` entries emitted as tombstones.
   3. `TailMergeExec` merges both streams by pk:
      - a durable key whose tail entry exists is dropped (it is already shadowed; the check is defensive);
      - tombstones are dropped;
      - output stops at `limit`.
   4. `fetch_rows` gives the documents. `next = Some(last pk)` when `limit` docs were returned and more remain (known by fetching `limit + 1`), else `None`.
7. **Get:**
   1. For each pk in request order, `tail.get(pk)`: `Present` → the tail doc; `Deleted` → `None`.
   2. The keys left go to `snapshot.get_by_pk` in one call (they cannot be shadowed: a shadowed key has a tail entry).
   3. Project, and return one entry per requested key, misses as `None` (S8).
8. **Count:** `FilterBitmapExec` cardinality over durable ∪ tail. No filter → `view.live_rows()`.
9. **`DocFetchExec` / `fetch_rows`:**
   - Durable row ids: one `dataset.take_rows(ids, ProjectionRequest::from_columns(["_pk", "_source" if needed, "_ingest_partition", "_ingest_offset", "_vector_i"…, "_sparse_j"…], dataset.schema()))`. Lance coalesces the reads. Columns absent from an older Lance schema (a vector added later) read as absent.
   - Tail row ids: from `tail.doc(row_id)`.
   - Output keeps the input order. A durable row id missing from the version is `Internal("row {id} is not in lance version {v}")`, since ids come from the same view.
10. **`filter_source`:**
    - `All` → the source; `None` → `None`.
    - `Paths { include, exclude }`, ES semantics: a pattern is a dotted path where `*` matches any run of characters, dots included. A key path *P* is included iff `include` is empty or some pattern matches *P* or a dotted prefix of *P*. Then *P* is excluded iff some `exclude` pattern matches *P* or a prefix of *P*.
    - Arrays are transparent: their elements are filtered by the same path. Objects left empty by `include` are removed.

11. **Constant-score text retrievers.** When a `Text` retriever's compiled query is `constant_score` and the effective sort is score-only, every match ties. The planner then computes its top k as the k smallest matching PKs: a k-way merge of `PkDictCache` cursors (rule 6's durable walk, checked against the query's `FilterBitmapExec` rows) with the tail's `keys_after`. It does not use `TantivySearchExec`. Scores are the constant; results equal the scoring path.

**Tests** (`tests/search.rs`):
- `constant_score_top_k_walks_pk_order`: a `Terms` query matching 5 000 docs, k = 10 → the 10 smallest PKs, equal to `TantivySearchExec`'s result.
- `rrf_golden_values` (M1.4 S1): lists A = [d1 0.9, d2 0.8, d3 0.7] and B = [d3 10, d1 5], k = 60 → d1 0.032522473, d3 0.03226646, d2 0.016129032 (each within 1e-7), order d1, d3, d2. With k = 1 → d1 0.8333334, d3 0.75, d2 0.33333334.
- `dbsf_golden_values` (M1.4 S2): the same lists → d1 1.0488155, d3 0.9511845, d2 0.50000006. A single-hit list [d4 3.0] fused with A gives d4 0.5.
- `dbsf_is_not_clamped`: a list with an outlier yields a normalized score above 1.
- `weighted_sum_golden_values`: weights [2.0, 0.5] → d3 6.4, d1 4.3, d2 1.6.
- `equal_scores_order_by_pk_on_every_path` (S3): ties in text, exact vector, fused and filter-only results all order by canonical PK (the `U64`s, then `Uuid`s, then `Str`s).
- `a_filter_only_request_is_in_pk_order` (S3).
- `rescore_scores_candidates_exactly` (S9), including a zero vector scoring 0.0.
- `hits_carry_requested_vectors_and_source` (S10): `vectors: ["a"]` returns exactly `a`; `vectors: ["a", "s"]` with `s` sparse returns `a` in `vectors` and `s` in `sparse_vectors`; `SourceFilter::All` → `Some`; `None` → `None`.
- `dense_and_sparse_retrievers_fuse`: the M1.6 fixture collection `sp` (steps 22–25): a `Sparse` retriever and a `Vector` retriever fused with `Rrf { k: 60 }` → pks 1, 2, 3 with RRF scores `2/61`, `1/62 + 1/63`, `1/62`; the same with `Dbsf` equals `fuse` over the two lists; a `Sparse` retriever as the input of `Rescore` rescores its candidates densely.
- `source_paths_filter_like_es`: include `["meta.*", "title"]`, exclude `["meta.secret"]` over a nested doc → the exact map.
- `search_after_prefix_skips_equal_values`; `search_after_with_the_pk_pages_exactly`: pages of 7 over 100 docs with 10 distinct sort values cover every doc exactly once.
- `offset_limit_and_threshold`; `total_hits_exact_up_to_and_none`.
- `group_by_groups_by_value`: 60 docs, 12 key values (strings and ints), `group_size 3`, `limit 5` → equals an in-test grouping of the exact ranking.
- `a_field_sort_with_a_vector_retriever_is_invalid`.
- `scroll_after_is_exclusive_and_ordered` (S8); `scroll_pages_cover_every_key_once_across_tail_and_splits`: 500 keys spread over 4 splits and the tail with updates and deletes; pages of 13 give every live key once, in PK order.
- `a_selective_filtered_scroll_sorts_the_matches`: equal results on both scroll paths.
- `get_returns_request_order_with_misses` (S8).
- `count_matches_the_exact_total`.

**Commit:** `query: add fusion, document fetch and tail merge`; `query: plan and execute search, get, count and scroll`.

### Task 8: Aggregations and highlighting

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/exec/aggs.rs`, `crates/operon-query/src/text/highlight.rs`, `crates/operon-query/tests/aggs.rs`.

**Consumes:**
- Tasks 5–7: `OpenSplit`, `SplitMask`, `RowSet`, `TantivySearchExec`, `fetch_rows`, `filter_source`. Task 2: `highlight_terms`, `QueryCompiler`.
- tantivy: `aggregation::agg_req::Aggregations`, `DistributedAggregationCollector::from_aggs(aggs, AggContextParams::new(AggregationLimitsGuard::new(Some(config.agg_memory_limit), Some(config.agg_bucket_limit)), tokenizer_manager()))`, `IntermediateAggregationResults::{merge_fruits, into_final_result}`, `SnippetGenerator::new(terms_text, analyzer, field, max_num_chars)`, `Snippet::{fragment, highlighted}`.
- Not the vendored `operon_quickwit::search::merge_intermediate_aggregation_result`: it merges postcard bytes, not in-memory results (row 0.51).

**Produces:**

```rust
// exec/aggs.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug)] pub enum AggDomain { Query { query: Query, filter: Option<Query> }, Filter(Option<Query>), Rows(RoaringTreemap) }
pub async fn aggregate(view: &ReadView, request: &serde_json::Value, domain: AggDomain, config: &SearchConfig)
    -> Result<serde_json::Value, ServiceError>;
/// A collector wrapper that skips masked docs and, for AggDomain::Rows, docs whose _rowid is not allowed.
pub struct MaskedCollector<C> { /* inner, per-segment masks */ }
impl<C: tantivy::collector::Collector> tantivy::collector::Collector for MaskedCollector<C> {}

// text/highlight.rs
pub fn highlight(schema: &CollectionSchema, view: &ReadView, request: &SearchRequest, stats: &GlobalStats,
                 hits: &[FetchedRow]) -> Result<Vec<BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>>, ServiceError>;
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Domain** (Ruling 6), chosen by the planner:
   - only a `Text` retriever, or field mode → `Query { query, filter }`;
   - no retrievers → `Filter(request.filter)`;
   - otherwise → `Rows(the fused candidate row ids after score_threshold)`.
2. **Request rewrite.** The JSON is walked recursively. For every node `{"top_hits": {…}}`:
   1. Read its `_source` option (`true`/absent → `All`, `false` → `None`, a list of includes, or `{"includes", "excludes"}` → `Paths`) and remove it.
   2. Remove `docvalue_fields` and remember them.
   3. Set `"docvalue_fields": ["_rowid"]`.

   The result is deserialized into `Aggregations`. A deserialization or validation error from Tantivy is `InvalidArgument("aggregations: {error}")`. A field that is not a fast field of the current schema is `InvalidArgument("aggregation field {f} is not a fast field")`, checked before running.
3. **Execution.** For each split in manifest order, then the tail, run `DistributedAggregationCollector` inside `MaskedCollector`, whose mask is:
   - the split's `SplitMask`, or the tail's non-live docs;
   - for `Rows`, also every doc whose `_rowid` is not in the set.

   The query is `Query`'s compiled `Bool{must:[query], filter:[filter]}`, the filter alone for `Filter`, and `AllQuery` for `Rows`. A split written before an aggregated field existed yields an empty intermediate result for it (Tantivy treats a missing column as no values).
4. **Merge** the intermediate results in that order with `IntermediateAggregationResults::merge_fruits`, in memory (row 0.51). Then `into_final_result(aggs, limits)`, and `serde_json::to_value`, which gives the ES-shaped JSON Quickwit serves. Size bound: each split's intermediate result is bounded by the collector's `AggregationLimitsGuard`, but nothing prunes the merged result to `segment_size` (Quickwit's `prune_intermediate_results` was not vendored), so a merged `terms` result holds up to Σ over splits of their buckets (at most splits × `agg_bucket_limit`) until `into_final_result` applies `size` and checks the bucket limit again.
5. **`top_hits` post-processing.** For every `top_hits` result, each hit's `docvalue_fields._rowid` is resolved with one `fetch_rows` for all hits of the response. Each hit becomes:
   - `{"_id": <pk JSON>, "_score": null, "_source": filter_source(source, remembered _source option)}` (omitted for `None`);
   - `"sort": <tantivy's sort values>`;
   - `"fields": {name: [values]}` for the remembered `docvalue_fields`, from the source through `field_values`.

   M1.5 relies on `_id`, `_score` and `_source` being present. Tantivy's `top_hits` sorts only by fast fields; hits that tie on every sort key come back in split then doc order (a documented limitation).
6. **Highlighting.** For each requested `HighlightField`:
   1. Get the query terms of that field from `highlight_terms` over every `Text` retriever's query.
   2. Weight each term by `1 / (1 + df_global)`, where `df_global` comes from the request's `GlobalStats` (computed if absent). This keeps fragment choice identical on every placement.
   3. Build `SnippetGenerator::new(terms, analyzer of the field, field, max_num_chars = fragment_size)`.
   4. Generate a snippet for each value of the field in the doc's source (`extract` at `source_path`).
   5. Render the snippet from `fragment()` and `highlighted()`: insert `pre_tag`/`post_tag` around each highlighted range, without HTML escaping (ES's default encoder). Values without a highlighted range are skipped.

   `number_of_fragments = 0` highlights each whole value (`max_num_chars` = its length). Otherwise the first `number_of_fragments` rendered snippets are kept, in value order. A non-`Text` field is `InvalidArgument("highlighting needs a text field: {f}")`.

**Tests** (`tests/aggs.rs`):
- `aggregations_over_splits_and_tail_equal_a_single_split`: the Task 5 fixtures A and B. `terms` on `tag`, `stats`, `histogram` and `range` on integer `n`, `cardinality` on `tag`, and `percentiles` on `n` are equal JSON on both.
- `deleted_and_shadowed_docs_are_not_aggregated`.
- `the_aggregation_domain_follows_the_retrievers`: a text query counts only its matches; a vector search counts only its candidates; filter-only counts the filter's matches.
- `top_hits_carry_id_score_and_source`: `terms tag → top_hits {size 2, sort [{n: desc}], _source: {includes: ["title"]}}` → each hit has `_id`, `_score: null` and `_source` with only `title`.
- `an_aggregation_over_a_field_added_later_skips_old_splits`.
- `an_invalid_aggregation_is_invalid_argument`; `a_non_fast_aggregation_field_is_invalid_argument`.
- `highlight_marks_query_terms_with_the_tags`: `match title "quick fox"` over `"The quick brown fox"` with `<b>`/`</b>` → `["The <b>quick</b> brown <b>fox</b>"]`.
- `number_of_fragments_zero_highlights_whole_values`.
- `highlights_are_identical_across_placements`: fixture A equals fixture B.

**Commit:** `query: add aggregations and highlighting`.

### Task 9: `CollectionService`

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/{service.rs,write.rs,catalog_cache.rs,lib.rs}`, `crates/operon-query/Cargo.toml` (feature `test-util`), `crates/operon-query/tests/{service.rs,common/mod.rs}`.

**Consumes:**
- Tasks 1–8.
- M1.1: `CollectionWriter::write`, `WriteOutcome`, `operon_collection::{OpResult, OpError, WriteError}`, `check_document`, `check_patch`, `unmapped_paths`, `propose_dynamic_fields`, `DynamicMappingError`, `apply_patch`, `retained_chain`, `live_manifest`, `CollectionConfig`.
- As built: `MetaStore::{create_namespace, create_collection, drop_collection, update_collection_schema, update_aliases, namespace_by_name, resolve_collection, collection, collections, aliases, collection_head, clock_ms, now_ms, watch_changes}` (M1.2a), called through `CollectionContext.meta: Arc<dyn MetaStore>`.

**Produces:**

```rust

pub struct ServiceConfig {
    pub default_partitions: u32,   // 4 (Ruling 20)
    pub hot_default: bool,         // true; the operon flag --hot=on|off
    pub tail: TailConfig, pub read: ReadConfig, pub search: SearchConfig, pub ann: AnnConfig, pub sql: SqlConfig,
    pub max_get_keys: usize,       // 10_000
    pub max_scroll_limit: usize,   // 10_000
    pub schema_retries: u32,       // 5
}
impl Default for ServiceConfig;
pub struct CollectionService { /* ctx, writer, reads, planner, hot: RwLock<Arc<dyn HotTier>>, placement: RwLock<(Arc<dyn Placement>, Arc<dyn RemoteReads>)>, catalog: CatalogCache, weak self */ }
impl fmt::Debug for CollectionService;   // finish_non_exhaustive
impl CollectionService {
    pub fn new(ctx: CollectionContext, writer: CollectionWriter, reader: LogReader, config: ServiceConfig) -> Arc<Self>;  // Arc::new_cyclic
    pub fn set_hot_tier(&self, hot: Arc<dyn HotTier>);
    pub fn set_placement(&self, placement: Arc<dyn Placement>, remote: Arc<dyn RemoteReads>);
    pub fn config(&self) -> &ServiceConfig;
    // overview §6.7, verbatim
    pub async fn ensure_namespace(&self, ns: &str) -> Result<(), ServiceError>;
    pub async fn create_collection(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, schema: CollectionSchema, partitions: Option<u32>) -> Result<CollectionInfo, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn drop_collection(&self, ns: &str, name: &str) -> Result<bool, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn get_collection(&self, ns: &str, name_or_alias: &str) -> Result<CollectionInfo, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn list_collections(&self, ns: &str) -> Result<Vec<CollectionInfo>, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn add_fields(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, fields: Vec<FieldSpec>, vectors: Vec<VectorSpec>, annotations: BTreeMap<String, String>) -> Result<CollectionSchema, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn update_aliases(&self, ns: &str, actions: Vec<AliasAction>) -> Result<(), ServiceError>;
    pub async fn write(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, ops: Vec<DocOp>, opts: WriteOptions) -> Result<WriteResult, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn get(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, pks: &[PrimaryKey], select: &Projection, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<Vec<Option<StoredDoc>>, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn search(&self, ns: &str, request: SearchRequest) -> Result<SearchResponse, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn count(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, filter: Option<Query>, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<u64, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn scroll(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, filter: Option<Query>, after: Option<PrimaryKey>, limit: usize, select: &Projection, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<(Vec<StoredDoc>, Option<PrimaryKey>), ServiceError>;
    pub async fn versions(&self, ns: &str, name: &str) -> Result<Vec<ManifestInfo>, ServiceError>;
    pub fn sql_context(&self, ns: &str) -> datafusion::prelude::SessionContext;   // body in Task 10
    // additions
    pub async fn pin(&self, ns: &str, name_or_alias: &str) -> Result<PinnedRead, ServiceError>;
    pub fn sql_context_with(&self, ns: &str, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> datafusion::prelude::SessionContext;   // Task 10
    pub async fn search_local(&self, ns: &str, request: SearchRequest) -> Result<SearchResponse, ServiceError>;     // never forwards
    pub async fn get_local(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, pks: &[PrimaryKey], select: &Projection, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<Vec<Option<StoredDoc>>, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn count_local(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, filter: Option<Query>, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<u64, ServiceError>;
    pub async fn scroll_local(&self, ns: &str, name: &str, filter: Option<Query>, after: Option<PrimaryKey>, limit: usize, select: &Projection, consistency: ReadConsistency) -> Result<(Vec<StoredDoc>, Option<PrimaryKey>), ServiceError>;

pub const CREATED_AT_ANNOTATION: &str = "operon.created_at_ms";
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] pub struct SqlConfig { pub max_rows: usize /* 10_000 */, pub timeout: Duration /* 30 s */, pub batch_rows: usize /* 8_192 */ }  // used by Task 10
// catalog_cache.rs: namespace name → sorted collection names, refreshed with namespaces/collections/aliases(Local, …) whenever watch_changes() completes (for sync SchemaProvider::table_names); Err(MetaStopped) ends the refresh task (row 0.79)
#[derive(Clone, Debug)] pub struct CatalogCache;
impl CatalogCache { pub fn start(meta: Arc<dyn MetaStore>) -> Self; pub fn names(&self, ns: &str) -> Vec<String>; }
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Entry of every read method** (`get`, `search`, `count`, `scroll`):
   1. `hot = hot::current().unwrap_or(RequestHot { enabled: config.hot_default, used: HotUsed::default() })`, captured before any spawn.
   2. Resolve the namespace name and the collection (name or alias) with `namespace_by_name(Local, ns)` and `resolve_collection(Local, ns_id, name_or_alias)`. An unknown namespace or collection → `NotFound { kind: "collection", name }` (S7: an absent namespace behaves like an empty one).
   3. `placement.owner(ns, cid)`: `Remote` → call the matching `RemoteReads` method with the *resolved collection name* and return its result. The implementation must forward `Operon-Hot` from `hot.enabled` and record the remote's `Operon-Hot-Used` into `hot.used` (documented on the trait). If the remote call returns `Unavailable` or `Timeout`, log it at `debug` and run the `*_local` path of step 4 instead (correctness never depends on the owner, §04 §5; M1.3 Ruling 14); any other error is returned as is.
   4. `Local` → the `*_local` path: `reads.view(…, hot_tier = if hot.enabled { current hot tier } else { Arc::new(NoHotTier) })`, then the planner.

   Validation (`validate_request`, `max_get_keys`, `max_scroll_limit`) runs before forwarding, so both sides reject the same requests.
2. **`ensure_namespace`:** `create_namespace(ns)`; the metastore validates the name (M0 `validate_name`, private to `operon-meta`, row 0.13): `ApplyError::InvalidArgument` → `InvalidArgument`, `NamespaceExists` is success. Every later metastore collection operation takes the `NamespaceId`, resolved with `namespace_by_name(Local, ns)`.
3. **`create_collection`:**
   1. `ensure_namespace`.
   2. `schema.version = 1`; `schema.annotations[CREATED_AT_ANNOTATION] = meta.now_ms()`.
   3. `create_collection(ns, name, schema, partitions or default_partitions)` on the `MetaStore`:
      - `CollectionExists(id)` → the existing collection's info;
      - `NameTaken` → read the existing collection. If its schema equals the request's ignoring `version` and `CREATED_AT_ANNOTATION`, and `partitions` is `None` or equal, return its info (retry-safe); else `AlreadyExists(name)`;
      - `InvalidArgument(m)` → `InvalidArgument(m)`.
4. **`drop_collection`:** `reads.stop_collection(cid)` (when resolvable), then the `MetaStore` `drop_collection` → `Some` → `true`, `None` → `false`.
5. **`get_collection` / `list_collections`:**
   - `collection_head(Local, cid)` gives the catalog record, the stream's high watermarks and the pointer; `aliases(Local, ns_id)` gives the aliases.
   - The live manifest comes through `ManifestCache`.
   - `CollectionInfo`:
     - `manifest_version` = pointer version (0 when absent);
     - `live_doc_count = manifest.live_doc_count`;
     - `size_bytes` = Σ `SplitRef.size_bytes` + Σ over the Lance dataset's fragments of the known `DataFile.file_size_bytes.get()` (unknown sizes count 0);
     - `created_at_ms` from the annotation (0 when absent);
     - `link_lag_records = Σ_p (hwm_p − applied_p)`;
     - `hot = current hot tier.status(ns, cid)`.
   - `list_collections` sorts by name. An absent namespace → `[]`.
6. **`add_fields`**, looping at most `schema_retries` times:
   1. `collection(Linearizable, cid)`.
   2. `next = schema + fields + vectors`, with `annotations` merged in (a new key overwrites). A field or vector whose name exists with an *identical* spec is skipped, so retries are idempotent; with a different spec it is `AlreadyExists("field {name}")`.
   3. `check_additive` → `IncompatibleSchema` becomes `InvalidArgument`.
   4. `update_collection_schema(cid, schema.version, next)`. `SchemaVersionMismatch` → retry.

   After the retries: `Unavailable("schema update raced {n} times")`. On success, `reads.notify(cid)`. No backfill (A4).
7. **`update_aliases`:** the `MetaStore` `update_aliases`:
   - `NameTaken(n)` → `AlreadyExists(n)`;
   - `UnknownCollection(n)` → `NotFound { kind: "collection", name: n }`;
   - `InvalidArgument` → `InvalidArgument`;
   - `NamespaceNotFound`, or a namespace name that does not resolve before the call (`update_aliases` takes a `NamespaceId`, row 0.13) → `NotFound { kind: "collection" }` when any action is a `Create`, else `Ok(())`.
8. **`write`** (`write.rs`):
   1. Resolve the collection (aliases allowed); `ops.len() > 10_000` → `InvalidArgument`.
   2. **Dynamic mapping** (Ruling 18): with `DynamicMapping::Map`, collect the unmapped paths of every Upsert document, Patch source and Patch `upsert` document (`unmapped_paths`). If any, run `propose_dynamic_fields(schema, sources)` and CAS the schema as in `add_fields` (with retries). `TooManyFields { limit }` → `SchemaViolation { field: first unmapped path, message: "mapping would exceed the field limit of {limit}" }`.
   3. **`atomic`:** validate every op against the schema read in step 2, in the order of M1.1's private `CollectionWriter` check (writer.rs:197-243, row 0.26): `PrimaryKey::validate`; for a patch, its `delete_keys` paths and an `upsert` whose pk differs from the patch's (M1.1 P25); `check_document` / `check_patch` (first violation); the encoded record size (`encode`, 16 MiB). The first failure fails the whole request with nothing written:
      - `SchemaViolation { field, message: "op {i}: {message}" }` for violations;
      - `InvalidArgument("op {i}: {message}")` for key, `delete_keys`, upsert-pk and size errors (a `check_patch` violation of field `delete_keys` counts as a `delete_keys` error).
   4. **`report_existence`:** read the current documents of the distinct keys with `get_local(…, Projection { source: All, vectors: every dense and sparse vector name, fields: [] }, Strong)`. Then simulate the ops in request order over that state *S* (Ruling 10). The results describe the request against *S*; the records themselves are folded later by the link in log order (M1.1 Task 10), where a patch of a missing key without `upsert` changes nothing, so a writer racing this request can make a result stale but never the stored state:
      - `Upsert(d)`: `Created` if `S(k)` is `None`, else `Updated`, also when `S(k) == d`: every upsert writes a new row and `seq_no` (M1.1 P32, row 0.53; ES `index` answers `updated`). Then `S(k) = d`.
      - `Delete`: `Deleted` if present, else `NotFound`. Then `S(k) = None`.
      - `Patch`:
        - absent with `upsert` → `Created`, `S = upsert`;
        - absent without → `NotFound`;
        - present → `n = apply_patch(Some(cur), op)`: `Noop` if `n == cur`, else `Updated`; `S = n`.
   5. `CollectionWriter::write(ns, cid, ops)`.
   6. Map op *i*:
      - `Written { partition, offset }` → `positions[i] = Some(OpPosition { partition, seq_no: offset })`, and `results[i]` = the simulated result (or `Accepted` without `report_existence`);
      - `Rejected(e)` → `Rejected(ServiceError)` with `OpError::InvalidArgument(m)` → `InvalidArgument(m)`, `SchemaViolation` → `SchemaViolation`, `DynamicMappingRequired { paths }` → `SchemaViolation { field: paths[0], message: "dynamic mapping required for {paths:?}" }`; `positions[i] = None`. A rejected op leaves *S* unchanged for later ops.

      Each partition's accepted ops are consecutive records in request order (one `append_many` batch per partition, M1.1), which M1.5 relies on for `_seq_no`.
   7. `reads.notify(cid)`.
   8. Return `WriteResult { token: outcome.token, results, positions }`.
9. **Error mapping** (Task 11 reuses it through `ServiceError`):
   - `WriteError::CollectionNotFound` → `NotFound { kind: "collection" }`; `TooManyOps` → `InvalidArgument`.
   - `LogError::{Backpressure, CommitUnknown, Closed, Store, Cache}` → `Unavailable`. The message for `CommitUnknown` says the outcome is unknown and that retrying keyed ops is safe. `LogError::InvalidArgument` → `InvalidArgument`; other `LogError`s → `Internal`.
   - `MetaError::{NotLeader, Timeout, Unavailable, ClockSkew}` → `Unavailable`.
   - `Rejected(ApplyError)`:
     - `InvalidArgument` and `IncompatibleSchema` → `InvalidArgument`;
     - `CollectionExists` and `NameTaken` → `AlreadyExists`;
     - `CollectionNotFound`, `UnknownCollection` and `NamespaceNotFound` → `NotFound { kind: "collection" }`;
     - `SchemaVersionMismatch` → `Unavailable` (callers retry);
     - anything else → `Internal`.
   - `CollectionError`: retryable → `Unavailable`, `ManifestGone` → `NotFound { kind: "pin" }`, `NotFound` → `NotFound { kind: "collection" }`, others → `Internal`.
10. **`versions`:**
    1. `live_manifest(Linearizable)`; no pointer → `[]`.
    2. `retained_chain(store, cache, live, config.keep_manifests, config.time_travel_retention, clock)`, with `clock` from `clock_ms(Linearizable)`.
    3. Map each manifest to `ManifestInfo { version, created_at_ms, size_bytes: Σ split size_bytes, live_doc_count, lance_version }`, in ascending version order.
11. **`pin`:** resolve the collection; `reads.pin(collection)` → `PinnedRead { collection: id, name, manifest_version, token }`.
12. **`shutdown`:** stop every tail and the catalog cache task.

**Tests** (`tests/service.rs`; `tests/common/mod.rs` holds `Fixture`: single-node meta, `FaultyStore(InMemory)`, `LogWriter`, `LogReader`, M1.1's link and index sources run on demand (`apply_link()`, through `operon_worker::run_once`) or continuously (`start_worker()`), and a `CollectionService`, with `fn service(&self) -> Arc<CollectionService>` and `async fn settle(&self)` that runs the sources until `link_lag_records == 0`):
- `create_collection_creates_the_namespace` (S7); `reads_in_an_absent_namespace_behave_as_empty`.
- `create_is_retry_safe_and_a_different_schema_is_already_exists`.
- `drop_frees_the_name_and_stops_the_tail`.
- `report_existence_answers_every_result`: a table of 12 op sequences, including repeated keys in one request (`upsert a`, `patch a`, `delete a`, `patch a` → `Created`, `Updated`, `Deleted`, `NotFound`), against the exact expected `results`.
- `an_atomic_write_rejects_the_whole_request`: one invalid op in 10 → `SchemaViolation` naming `op 7`, and nothing is written (all high watermarks unchanged).
- `a_non_atomic_write_reports_rejected_ops_and_writes_the_rest`.
- `accepted_ops_are_consecutive_per_partition` (M1.5): 30 ops on 4 partitions → per partition, the `seq_no`s in request order are consecutive.
- `dynamic_map_mode_adds_fields_before_writing`: the schema version increases and the new doc is searchable by the new field at once.
- `add_fields_is_additive_idempotent_and_not_backfilled` (A4, replacing M1.4 S6): old docs do not match a term on the new field; new docs do.
- `aliases_resolve_everywhere_and_map_errors`.
- `versions_list_the_retained_manifests_in_order`.
- `get_collection_reports_manifest_version_and_link_lag` (M1.7 A3): write 7 ops without applying → `link_lag_records == 7`; `settle` → 0, and `manifest_version` increased.
- `a_remote_owner_forwards_every_read`: a fake `Placement` says `Remote`; a recording `RemoteReads` receives `search`, `get`, `count` and `scroll` with the resolved name, and the local planner is not used.
- `an_unavailable_remote_owner_falls_back_to_a_local_read`: the recording `RemoteReads` answers `Unavailable` (then `Timeout`) → each read returns the local result; an `InvalidArgument` from it is returned unchanged.
- `operon_hot_off_uses_no_hot_tier`: with a counting fake `HotTier`, `hot::scope(RequestHot { enabled: false, .. }, service.search(..))` makes no call to the fake, and `record_access` is not counted.
- `pin_returns_the_current_manifest_and_high_watermarks`.
- `sparse_collections_round_trip_through_the_service`: create with sparse vectors `s` (`Idf`) and `t`; write upserts and patches carrying them; `get` with `vectors: ["s"]` returns `s` only; `report_existence` answers `Updated` for an upsert that repeats a doc including its sparse vector (P32), `Noop` for a patch that sets a sparse vector to its current value, and `Updated` for a patch that changes only a sparse weight; `add_fields` cannot add a sparse vector (the schema's `sparse_vectors` are unchanged, and `UpdateCollectionSchema` with a new one is `InvalidArgument`).

**Commit:** `query: add CollectionService`.

### Task 10: SQL — the DataFusion catalog, `CollectionProvider` and the search table functions

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/sql/{mod.rs,catalog.rs,provider.rs,udtf.rs,exprs.rs,json_rows.rs}`, `crates/operon-query/tests/sql.rs`.

**Consumes:**
- Task 9: `CollectionService`, `CatalogCache`. Tasks 5–7: `FilterBitmapExec`, `RowSet`, `fetch_rows`.
- M1.1: `extract`, `coerce`, `IndexValue`.
- DataFusion 54.1:
  - `CatalogProvider`, `SchemaProvider` (async `table`, sync `table_names`), `TableProvider::{scan, supports_filters_pushdown}`, `TableProviderFilterPushDown::{Inexact, Unsupported}`;
  - `TableFunctionImpl::call_with_args(TableFunctionArgs)`, `SessionContext::{new_with_config, register_catalog, register_udtf, register_udf, sql_with_options}`, `SessionConfig::with_default_catalog_and_schema`, `SQLOptions`;
  - `ScalarUDFImpl` with `Volatility::Immutable` (the nested retriever calls of `rrf`, rule 5);
  - `ExprSimplifier::new(SimplifyContext::default()).simplify(expr)`, which evaluates immutable scalar functions of literal arguments (its `ConstEvaluator`; verify for UDFs at execution).
- Lance: `scan().with_row_addr_prefilter(..).project(..).with_row_id().try_into_stream()`.

**Produces:**

```rust
// SqlConfig is defined in Task 9 (service.rs)
pub const COLLECTIONS_SCHEMA: &str = "collections";
pub fn read_only_options() -> datafusion::execution::context::SQLOptions;   // DDL, DML and statements disallowed
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct NamespaceCatalog;   impl CatalogProvider for NamespaceCatalog {}      // one schema: "collections"
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct CollectionsSchema;  impl SchemaProvider for CollectionsSchema {}
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct CollectionProvider; impl TableProvider for CollectionProvider {}
pub fn collection_arrow_schema(schema: &CollectionSchema) -> SchemaRef;      // rule 2
pub fn expr_to_query(expr: &datafusion::prelude::Expr, schema: &CollectionSchema) -> Option<Query>;   // rule 4
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct VectorSearchFunction; #[derive(Debug)] pub struct TextSearchFunction; #[derive(Debug)] pub struct HybridSearchFunction;
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct RrfFunction; #[derive(Debug)] pub struct RerankFunction;   // Ruling 23 (D56)
impl TableFunctionImpl for VectorSearchFunction {} /* and the other four */
/// Scalar forms of `vector_search` and `text_search`, used only as `rrf` arguments: each returns a retriever descriptor (Utf8 JSON, rule 5).
#[derive(Debug)] pub struct RetrieverDescriptorUdf { /* "vector_search" | "text_search" */ }
impl ScalarUDFImpl for RetrieverDescriptorUdf {}   // Volatility::Immutable
pub const SQL_SEARCH_FUNCTIONS: [&str; 5] = ["vector_search", "text_search", "hybrid_search", "rrf", "rerank"];
pub const SQL_DEFAULT_K: usize = 1_000;   // Spice's text_search default
pub struct SqlResult { pub schema: SchemaRef, pub batches: Vec<RecordBatch>, pub truncated: bool }
pub async fn run_read_only(ctx: &SessionContext, sql: &str, config: &SqlConfig) -> Result<SqlResult, ServiceError>;
pub fn rows_to_json(result: &SqlResult) -> serde_json::Value;                // {"columns": [...], "rows": [...], "truncated": bool}
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Context.** `sql_context_with(ns, consistency)`:
   - `SessionContext::new_with_config(SessionConfig::new().with_default_catalog_and_schema(ns, "collections").with_information_schema(true))`;
   - `register_catalog(ns, NamespaceCatalog)`;
   - `register_udtf` for every name in `SQL_SEARCH_FUNCTIONS`, and `register_udf` for the two `RetrieverDescriptorUdf`s (`vector_search`, `text_search`; DataFusion keeps table and scalar functions in separate registries, verify).

   No URL tables and no file-reading table functions. `sql_context(ns)` = `sql_context_with(ns, Strong)`.

   Tables resolve as `kb`, `collections.kb` or `"<ns>".collections.kb`:
   - `CollectionsSchema::table_names()` = `CatalogCache::names(ns)`;
   - `table(name)` resolves the name or alias with a fresh `resolve_collection(Local, …)`, so a table created after the context exists is found.

   Every scan resolves its `ReadView` when it executes, with the context's consistency and the hot scope captured at `sql_context_with`.
2. **Table columns** (`collection_arrow_schema`), in this order:
   - `_id` Utf8, not null: a `U64` in decimal, a `Str` as itself, a `Uuid` lowercase hyphenated;
   - `_source` Utf8 (JSON text), not null, converted from Lance's `_source` `Binary` (UTF-8 JSON bytes, row 0.28);
   - `_seq_no` UInt64, `_partition` UInt32;
   - one column per field, named `FieldSpec.name`:
     - Text, Keyword and Uuid → Utf8;
     - I64 → Int64; F64 → Float64; Bool → Boolean;
     - Date → `Timestamp(Millisecond, Some("UTC"))`;
     - Json → Utf8 (the JSON text of the value at `source_path`);
   - one column per vector, named `VectorSpec.name` (`_vector` for the unnamed `""`): `FixedSizeList(Float32, dim)`, nullable;
   - one column per sparse vector, named `SparseVectorSpec.name`: the Lance `_sparse_<j>` type `Struct<indices: List<UInt32>, values: List<Float32>>`, nullable. SQL has no sparse search table function in M1 (sparse search is reached through `POST …/query` and the Qdrant API).

   A field's column holds its *first* extracted value (`extract` + `coerce`, in extraction order), or null. Multi-valued fields are fully available through `_source`.
3. **Scan** (`CollectionProvider::scan(state, projection, filters, limit)`) returns a `CollectionScanExec`. Its execution:
   1. `view`.
   2. When `filters` convert (`expr_to_query`), their conjunction runs through `FilterBitmapExec`.
   3. A Lance scan of the needed columns (`_pk`, `_source` whenever any field column or `_source` is projected, `_ingest_*`, the projected vectors) with `with_row_id()`, in batches of `batch_rows`. The mask is `from_allowed(A_d ∖ shadow)`, or `from_block(shadow)` without a filter.
   4. The tail's live docs (in `A_t` when filtered).
   5. Stop after `limit` rows when given.

   Row order is unspecified (durable, then tail). `supports_filters_pushdown` answers `Inexact` for filters `expr_to_query` converts, else `Unsupported`, so DataFusion always re-applies the filter to the first-value columns.
4. **`expr_to_query`** (anything else gives `None`):
   - `col = lit`, `lit = col` → `Term`; `col IN (lits)` → `Terms`.
   - `col >`/`>=`/`<`/`<=` `lit` → `Range`; `col BETWEEN a AND b` → `Range gte/lte`.
   - `col IS NOT NULL` → `Exists`; `col IS NULL` → `IsEmpty`.
   - `AND` / `OR` / `NOT` → `Bool` must / should / must_not.
   - `_id = 'x'` → `Ids([Str("x")])`, plus `U64(x)` when `x` is a canonical decimal, plus `Uuid` when `x` parses as one. (The display form is ambiguous; DataFusion's re-filter keeps SQL semantics.)

   Literal conversion: Utf8 → `Str`; integers → `I64`/`U64`; floats → `F64`; Boolean → `Bool`; `Timestamp` → `Date` (µs). Only columns that are collection fields convert (not `_source`, `_seq_no`, vectors).
5. **Table functions** (Rulings 8 and 23; Spice's names and argument order, D56). Arguments are positional. Each is simplified with `ExprSimplifier` and must be a literal (`Expr::Literal(value, _)`), else `plan_err!("argument {i} of {f} must be a constant")`. A query vector is a `List`/`FixedSizeList` literal of numbers (the planner constant-folds `[0.1, 0.2]` / `make_array`) or a Utf8 JSON array. An optional argument may be given as `NULL` to skip it.
   - `vector_search(collection, query_vector [, field [, k [, filter_json [, exact]]]])` → `Retriever::Vector { field, query, k, params: { exact }, filter }`. `field` absent or `NULL`: the collection's only dense vector, else `plan_err!("vector_search on {c} needs a field: it has {n} vectors")`. `k` defaults to `SQL_DEFAULT_K` (1 000).
   - `text_search(collection, text [, field [, k [, filter_json]]])` → `Retriever::Text { query: Match { field, text, operator: Or, .. }, k }`. `field` absent or `NULL`: the collection's only Text field, else `plan_err!("text_search on {c} needs a field: it has {n} text fields")`. `k` defaults to 1 000.
   - `hybrid_search(collection, vector_field, query_vector, text_field, text, k [, fusion ('rrf' | 'dbsf'), [rrf_k (60)], [filter_json]])` → both retrievers with `Fusion::Rrf { k: rrf_k }` or `Dbsf`, and `limit = k`. This is Operon's shorthand for `rrf(vector_search(…), text_search(…))` with explicit fields; it is unchanged.
   - `rrf(retriever, retriever [, …] [, k [, limit]])` → `SearchRequest { retrievers, fusion: Some(Fusion::Rrf { k }), limit }`. Each `retriever` is a nested `vector_search(…)` or `text_search(…)` call with the arguments above. In argument position the name resolves to its `RetrieverDescriptorUdf`, which `ExprSimplifier` folds into a Utf8 literal `{"collection": c, "retriever": <Retriever JSON>}` (field defaults are resolved when `rrf` plans, against the collection's schema). At least two retrievers; all must name the same collection (`plan_err!("rrf fuses retrievers of one collection, got {a} and {b}")`). Trailing numeric literals are `k` (default 60; an integer, or a float with no fraction, as Spice's `60.0`; else `plan_err!`) and `limit` (default: the largest retriever `k`, at most `max_window`). A descriptor evaluated anywhere else is just its JSON text.
   - `rerank(…)` with any arguments → `plan_err!("rerank arrives in M3 (D56); use rrf or hybrid_search")`. It is registered so the name is reserved and the error is clear.

   If execution shows that DataFusion 54 cannot plan the nested calls (the SQL planner resolves them before `rrf` sees them, or the simplifier does not fold UDFs), `rrf` instead takes each retriever as a Utf8 JSON literal (`rrf('{"collection": "kb", "retriever": {"vector": …}}', …)`); record it as an execution row, and Ruling 23's names stay.

   `filter_json` is a Utf8 literal holding an IR `Query` in its JSON form. The provider's columns are `_id`, `_score` (Float32; the fused score for `rrf` and `hybrid_search`), then the collection columns of rule 2. Its scan runs `search_local` (or the forwarded `search`) at execution and emits the hits in rank order.
6. **`run_read_only`:**
   1. `ctx.sql_with_options(sql, read_only_options())`: a refused plan → `InvalidArgument("only read-only queries are allowed: {error}")`; other planning errors → `InvalidArgument("sql: {error}")`.
   2. `execute_stream`, collecting until `max_rows` rows (`truncated = true` when more exist; the last batch is sliced).
   3. `timeout` → `Timeout`.
7. **`rows_to_json`:** `{"columns": [{"name", "type": format!("{}", data_type)}], "rows": [[…]], "truncated": bool}`, rows in column order:
   - null → `null`; Boolean → bool;
   - Int* and UInt* → numbers (u64 exact); floats → numbers, with non-finite → `null`;
   - Decimal → string;
   - Utf8/LargeUtf8/Utf8View → string; Binary* → standard base64;
   - Date32/64 → `YYYY-MM-DD`; Timestamp → RFC 3339 at the unit's precision with `Z`;
   - List/LargeList/FixedSizeList → arrays; Struct → objects; Map → `[{"key", "value"}]`; Dictionary → its values;
   - anything else → arrow's `ArrayFormatter` display string.

**Tests** (`tests/sql.rs`, the service fixture):
- `select_count_star_counts_durable_and_tail_rows`: 3 committed docs plus 2 in the tail → `SELECT count(*) AS n FROM kb` → `{"columns":[{"name":"n","type":"Int64"}],"rows":[[5]],"truncated":false}` (the M1.6 fixture shape).
- `typed_columns_come_from_source`; `a_multi_valued_field_exposes_its_first_value`.
- `pushed_down_filters_equal_unpushed_results`: 20 random WHERE clauses → equal row sets with pushdown on and with pushdown disabled (a test flag on the provider).
- `vector_search_text_search_and_hybrid_search_match_the_ir`: each table function returns the same pks and scores as `search`, with every argument given and with `field` and `k` omitted on a collection with one vector and one text field.
- `omitted_fields_need_a_single_candidate`: `vector_search('kb2', [..])` on a collection with two vectors, and `text_search('kb2', 'x')` with two text fields, give the exact planning errors.
- `rrf_fuses_nested_search_calls_like_the_ir`: `SELECT _id, _score FROM rrf(vector_search('kb', [1.0, 0.0, 0.0], 'embedding', 10), text_search('kb', 'refund', 'body', 10)) LIMIT 3` returns the M1.6 fixture step 12 ranking (pks 1, 3, 2) with the scores of `search` with `Fusion::Rrf { k: 60 }`; `, 60.0` and `, 60` as a trailing `k` give the same result, `, 60.5` and a retriever of another collection are planning errors.
- `rerank_is_reserved_for_m3`: `SELECT * FROM rerank(rrf(…))` → `InvalidArgument` whose message contains `rerank arrives in M3`.
- `a_non_constant_table_function_argument_is_refused`.
- `ddl_and_dml_are_refused`: `CREATE TABLE`, `INSERT`, `COPY` and `SET` → `InvalidArgument`.
- `tables_resolve_by_name_alias_and_qualified_name`.
- `results_are_capped_and_marked_truncated`.
- `rows_to_json_formats_every_type`: a table of 18 Arrow types.

**Commit:** `query: add the SQL catalog and the search table functions`.

### Task 11: Native REST API, error-mapping fixes and server wiring

**Files:**
- `crates/operon/Cargo.toml`: deps `operon-query`, `operon-collection`, `tower`, `http`; feature `flight` in `default`.
- `crates/operon/src/api.rs` → `crates/operon/src/api/mod.rs` (a `git mv`), plus `crates/operon/src/api/{collections.rs,query.rs,sql.rs,errors.rs}`.
- `crates/operon/src/{server.rs,main.rs}`.
- `crates/operon/tests/it/{native_collections.rs,native_query.rs,native_sql.rs,http.rs}` (row 0.89).
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: the metastore boundary grep names `crates/operon/src/api` instead of `crates/operon/src/api.rs`, which the move removes (row 0.87).

**Consumes:** Tasks 1, 9 and 10. The as-built `AppState`, `router`, `ApiError`, `From<MetaError>`/`From<LogError> for ApiError` (`api.rs:37-221`), and `ServerConfig`/`Server::start`/`main.rs` `Tuning`. M1.1: `Server::collection_context()`, `Server::log_writer()`, `CONSISTENCY_TOKEN_HEADER`.

**Produces:**

```rust
// api/mod.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AppState { pub meta: Arc<dyn MetaStore> /* M1.2a */, pub writer: LogWriter, pub reader: LogReader, pub store: Store,
                      pub registry: TargetRegistry /* M1.1 */, pub collections: Arc<CollectionService> }
pub fn router(state: AppState) -> Router;           // M0 routes + the routes below, wrapped in HotLayer::new(state.collections.config().hot_default)
// api/errors.rs
impl From<ServiceError> for ApiError;               // rule 3
// server.rs
pub struct ServerConfig { /* existing */ pub query: ServiceConfig, pub flight_sql: Option<SocketAddr> /* None in ServerConfig::new */ }
impl Server {
    pub fn collections(&self) -> Arc<CollectionService>;
    pub fn flight_sql_addr(&self) -> Option<SocketAddr>;      // Task 12
}
// main.rs (dev and standalone): --flight-sql-listen <addr> (dev 127.0.0.1:8082, standalone 0.0.0.0:8082), --no-flight-sql, --hot <on|off> (on);
// hidden dev tuning: --tail-max-bytes <N>, --consistency-wait-ms <N>
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Routes.**
   - The table below lists every route, its request and its response. Bodies are JSON, with the JSON forms of Task 1. `{c}` is a collection name, or an alias where noted.
   - Every write response carries `Operon-Consistency-Token: <token>`; every read response carries it with its `read_token`. (`CONSISTENCY_TOKEN_HEADER` is mixed case; the handlers use `HeaderName::from_static("operon-consistency-token")`, row 0.21.)
   - A read accepts the `Operon-Consistency-Token` request header. It overrides a `"strong"` or absent body consistency, and an `"eventual"` one, with `AtLeast(header token)`. A body `at_least` token is merged into it (`ConsistencyToken::merge`); a `pinned` body wins.

   | Method, path | Request | Success |
   |---|---|---|
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections` | `{"name", "schema": Schema, "partitions"?: u32}` | 201 `CollectionInfo` (also for a retry-safe repeat) |
   | `GET /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections` | — | 200 `{"collections": [CollectionInfo]}` sorted by name |
   | `GET /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections/{c}` (alias ok) | — | 200 `CollectionInfo` (M1.3 Task 8 replaces its `hot` value with the owner's full status) |
   | `DELETE /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections/{c}` | — | 200 `{"dropped": bool}` |
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections/{c}/fields` | `{"fields"?: [Field], "vectors"?: [Vector], "annotations"?: {}}` | 200 `{"schema": Schema}` |
   | `GET /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections/{c}/versions` | — | 200 `{"versions": [ManifestInfo]}` |
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/aliases` | `{"actions": [{"create": {"alias", "collection"}} \| {"delete": {"alias"}}]}` | 200 `{}` |
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/collections/{c}/documents` (alias ok) | `{"ops": [Op], "report_existence"?: false}` | 200 `{"token", "results": [str], "positions": [{"partition", "seq_no"} \| null]}` |
   | `POST …/collections/{c}/documents/get` | `{"ids": [Id], "select"?: Projection, "consistency"?: Consistency}` | 200 `{"documents": [StoredDoc \| null], "read_token"}` |
   | `POST …/collections/{c}/documents/scroll` | `{"filter"?: Query, "after"?: Id, "limit"?: 100, "select"?: Projection, "consistency"?}` | 200 `{"documents": [StoredDoc], "next": Id \| null, "read_token"}` |
   | `POST …/collections/{c}/documents/count` | `{"filter"?: Query, "consistency"?}` | 200 `{"count": u64, "read_token"}` |
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/query` | `SearchRequest` or the §05 §4 body (Task 1 rule 8) | 200 `SearchResponse` |
   | `POST /v1/namespaces/{ns}/sql` | `{"query": str, "consistency"?}` | 200 `{"columns", "rows", "truncated"}` |
   | `POST …/collections/{c}/scan` (alias ok; added by Task 14, not here) | `{"at"?: "current" \| {"manifest_version"} \| {"token"}}` | 200 `ScanPlan` |

   - Op: `{"upsert": {"id", "source"?: {}, "vectors"?: {}, "sparse_vectors"?: {name: {"indices", "values"}}}}`, `{"delete": {"id"}}`, or `{"patch": {"id", "mode"?: "merge_deep"|"merge_top"|"replace", "source"?: {}, "delete_keys"?: [], "vectors"?: {name: [f32] | null}, "sparse_vectors"?: {name: {"indices", "values"} | null}, "upsert"?: Doc | null}}` (overview A30). An invalid sparse vector is `InvalidArgument("op {i}: sparse vector {name}: {error}")`.
   - `results` strings: `created`, `updated`, `deleted`, `not_found`, `noop`, `accepted`.
   - StoredDoc JSON: `{"id", "source": {…} | null, "vectors": {…}, "sparse_vectors"?: {…} (omitted when empty), "fields": {…}, "seq_no", "partition"}`.
   - Writes are atomic (Ruling 16): the handler calls `write` with `atomic: true`. A rejected request is the error of the first failing op, with extra `"index": i`.
2. **`M1.6`'s W-table** is satisfied as written, with these deviations for its Task 0:
   - PK sort values are plain JSON keys (a uuid as `{"uuid": …}`);
   - `StoredDoc` and `WriteResult` carry extra keys;
   - W13 adds `"truncated"`;
   - the error codes are the ones of rule 3.
3. **Errors:**
   - `ServiceError` → `{status: http_status(), code: code(), message}` plus `kind`/`name` or `field`. `Unavailable` adds the header `Retry-After: 1`.
   - The M0 mapping changes (Ruling 17; `api.rs:162-201` already has the M1.1 rows below, row 0.58, so only the first two bullets and `Retry-After` are new): in `From<MetaError>`,
     - `ApplyError::{VersionMismatch, Fenced, LeaseHeld, LeaseLost, SchemaVersionMismatch}` → 409 `conflict`;
     - `ApplyError::{StaleObject, StaleCommit, IndexMismatch}` → 503 `unavailable`;
     - M1.1's `CollectionExists`/`NameTaken` → 409 `already_exists`;
     - `CollectionNotFound`/`UnknownCollection` → 404;
     - `IncompatibleSchema` → 400.

     Every 503, M0's included, gets `Retry-After: 1`.
   - `Operon-Hot` parse errors are 400 through `HotLayer`.
   - Malformed JSON is 400 `invalid_argument` (`bad request body: …`), as in M0.
4. **Produce onto implicit streams** (Ruling 17). `POST …/streams/{s}/partitions/{p}/records` with `s` starting `_collection.`:
   1. Resolve the stream's collection through `Collection.stream`.
   2. Every record must have a key that `PrimaryKey::from_canonical` accepts with `partition_of(pk, partitions) == p`. Otherwise 400 `invalid_argument` `"record {i}: key does not belong to partition {p}"`.
5. **Server wiring:**
   1. In `start_on`, after M1.1's collection context and before `api::router`: `collections = CollectionService::new(ctx.clone(), CollectionWriter::new(meta_store.clone(), writer.clone()), reader.clone(), config.query.clone())` (`meta_store: Arc<dyn MetaStore>`, row 0.88), with the `reader` `start_on` already builds (`Server` keeps none, row 0.52).
   2. `AppState.collections = collections.clone()`, and `router` is wrapped in `HotLayer`.
   3. Flight SQL (Task 12, with Task 13's `DoPut` ingest) starts after the HTTP listener when `config.flight_sql` is `Some` and the `flight` feature is on.
   4. Shutdown order: stop HTTP accept → stop Flight → `collections.shutdown()` → the M1.1 order (writer, worker, factory close, cache, node).
6. **CLI:**
   - `operon dev` sets `flight_sql = Some(127.0.0.1:8082)` and `operon standalone` sets `Some(0.0.0.0:8082)`, unless `--no-flight-sql`. `--flight-sql-listen` overrides the address.
   - `--hot on|off` (also accepted as `--hot=on|off`) sets `query.hot_default`. This plan owns the flag; M1.3 Task 8 also maps it to `HotTierConfig.enabled` and adds `--hot-pin-all`.
   - Once the Flight listener is bound, the binary prints `operon flight sql listening on grpc://<addr>` to stdout (M1.6 W14, M1.7 A4). The M0 line `operon listening on http://<addr>` is unchanged.

**Tests:**
- `crates/operon/tests/native_collections.rs`:
  - `collection_routes_speak_the_documented_json`: create (201), repeat (201, the same `id`), a different schema (409 `already_exists`), list, get by alias, versions and drop twice (`dropped` true, then false), each against its exact JSON keys.
  - `write_get_scroll_count_round_trip_over_http`: the M1.6 fixture steps 10–16, including id `18446744073709551615`, `"k-str"` and `{"uuid": …}`; get in request order with `null` for 999; patch and delete visible to the next get with no token.
  - `a_rejected_atomic_write_is_400_with_the_op_index`.
  - `write_responses_carry_the_consistency_token_header`.
- `crates/operon/tests/native_query.rs`:
  - `hybrid_search_over_http_returns_the_fixture_ranking` (M1.6 fixture 12: pks 1, 3, 2);
  - `a_filter_only_query_over_http` (fixture 13);
  - `sparse_and_hybrid_queries_over_http` (M1.6 fixture steps 22–26: the sparse-only ranking pks 1, 2 with scores 2.0 and 0.5 and no third hit, the dense + sparse RRF ranking pks 1, 2, 3, and `sparse_vectors` in `documents/get`);
  - `the_section_05_body_is_accepted`;
  - `operon_hot_used_is_none_without_a_hot_tier_and_off_is_honoured`;
  - `an_invalid_operon_hot_header_is_400`.
- `crates/operon/tests/native_sql.rs`: `sql_over_http_returns_columns_and_rows` (fixture 17); `sql_ddl_is_400`.
- `crates/operon/tests/http.rs`:
  - `meta_conflicts_map_to_409_and_stale_objects_to_503`: a unit table over `From<MetaError> for ApiError` covering every `ApplyError` variant, including `Retry-After`.
  - `a_record_for_the_wrong_partition_of_an_implicit_stream_is_rejected`.
  - `the_dev_binary_prints_the_flight_sql_line` (child process, `--flight-sql-listen 127.0.0.1:0`).

**Commit:** `operon: serve the native collection, query and SQL API`; `operon: map metastore conflicts to 409 and stale objects to 503`.

### Task 12: Arrow Flight SQL

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/flight.rs`, `crates/operon-query/tests/flight.rs`, `crates/operon/src/server.rs` (listener), `crates/operon/tests/flight_sql.rs`.

**Consumes:**
- Task 10: `sql_context_with`, `read_only_options`, `run_read_only`, `CatalogCache`. Task 1: `HotLayer`, `ServiceError`.
- `arrow-flight 58.4` (`flight-sql-experimental`):
  - `sql::server::FlightSqlService` with `get_flight_info_statement`, `do_get_statement`, `get_flight_info_catalogs`/`do_get_catalogs`, `get_flight_info_schemas`/`do_get_schemas`, `get_flight_info_tables`/`do_get_tables`, `get_flight_info_table_types`/`do_get_table_types`, `get_flight_info_sql_info`/`do_get_sql_info`, `do_handshake`, `register_sql_info`;
  - `sql::metadata::{GetCatalogsBuilder, GetDbSchemasBuilder, GetTablesBuilder, SqlInfoDataBuilder}`, `sql::{CommandStatementQuery, TicketStatementQuery, CommandGetCatalogs, CommandGetDbSchemas, CommandGetTables, SqlInfo}`;
  - `flight_service_server::FlightServiceServer`, `encode::FlightDataEncoderBuilder`, `FlightInfo`, `FlightEndpoint`, `Ticket`.
- `tonic 0.14` `transport::Server`.

**Produces:**

```rust
pub const NAMESPACE_METADATA: &str = "operon-namespace";          // default namespace "default" when absent
pub const TICKET_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b"OPFS";  pub const TICKET_VERSION: u16 = 1;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct StatementTicket { pub namespace: String, pub query: String, pub token: Option<String> }
pub fn encode_ticket(t: &StatementTicket) -> Bytes;  pub fn decode_ticket(b: &[u8]) -> Result<StatementTicket, ServiceError>;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] pub struct FlightConfig { pub max_duration: Duration /* 10 min */ }
#[derive(Clone)] pub struct OperonFlightSql { /* Arc<CollectionService>, FlightConfig */ }
impl OperonFlightSql { pub fn new(service: Arc<CollectionService>, config: FlightConfig) -> Self; }
#[tonic::async_trait] impl FlightSqlService for OperonFlightSql { type FlightService = Self; /* rule 2 */ }
pub async fn serve_flight_sql(listener: tokio::net::TcpListener, service: Arc<CollectionService>, config: FlightConfig,
                              shutdown: CancellationToken) -> Result<(), tonic::transport::Error>;
```

**Semantics:**
1. **Ticket format** (Operon envelope): `b"OPFS" | u16 LE 1 | postcard(StatementTicket) | crc32c u32 LE` over every preceding byte. `decode_ticket` rejects a wrong magic, an unknown version, a bad crc or trailing bytes with `InvalidArgument("invalid statement ticket")`. The ticket is self-contained, so any node can serve `DoGet`.
2. **Requests:**
   - The namespace comes from metadata `operon-namespace` (default `default`).
   - The consistency is `AtLeast(token)` when metadata `operon-consistency-token` parses, else `Strong`.
   - `operon-hot` goes through `HotLayer` on the tonic server (`Server::builder().layer(HotLayer::new(..))`).
3. **Statements:**
   - `get_flight_info_statement(CommandStatementQuery { query, .. })`:
     1. Plan `query` with `sql_context_with(ns, consistency).sql_with_options(query, read_only_options())` to get its schema.
     2. Return `FlightInfo` with that schema, one endpoint whose ticket is `TicketStatementQuery { statement_handle: encode_ticket(..) }` (no location, so the client reuses this connection), and `total_records = -1`.
   - `do_get_statement`:
     1. Decode the ticket.
     2. Plan again and execute as a stream under `max_duration`.
     3. Encode with `FlightDataEncoderBuilder`.

     Nothing is row-capped here (Flight is for bulk results).
4. **Metadata:**
   - `GetCatalogs`: every namespace name, sorted.
   - `GetDbSchemas`: `(ns, "collections")` for every namespace matching `catalog` and `db_schema_filter_pattern` (SQL `LIKE`, via the builder).
   - `GetTables`: `(ns, "collections", name, "TABLE")` for every collection of the matching namespaces, with the Arrow schema of rule 2 of Task 10 when `include_schema` is set.
   - `GetTableTypes`: `["TABLE"]`.
   - `GetSqlInfo`:
     - `FlightSqlServerName = "operon"`;
     - `FlightSqlServerVersion` = the crate version;
     - `FlightSqlServerArrowVersion = "58.4"`;
     - `FlightSqlServerReadOnly = true`;
     - `FlightSqlServerTransaction` = none (0).
   - `do_handshake` answers an empty `HandshakeResponse` (no authentication in M1, §6.9).
   - Bulk ingestion (`CommandStatementIngest`) and path-descriptor `DoPut` are Task 13. Prepared statements, updates and transactions answer `Unimplemented`.
5. **Errors:** `ServiceError` → `tonic::Status`:
   - `NotFound` → `NotFound`;
   - `AlreadyExists` → `AlreadyExists`;
   - `InvalidArgument` and `SchemaViolation` → `InvalidArgument`;
   - `Unavailable` → `Unavailable`;
   - `Timeout` → `DeadlineExceeded`;
   - `Internal` → `Internal`.

   Messages are the `Display` text.

**Tests:**
- `crates/operon-query/tests/flight.rs`:
  - `tickets_round_trip_and_reject_corruption` (proptest round trip; a flipped byte and a wrong version are rejected).
  - `sql_info_declares_read_only`.
- `crates/operon/tests/flight_sql.rs` (an in-process `Server` with `flight_sql = 127.0.0.1:0`, and arrow-flight's `FlightSqlServiceClient` over a tonic channel):
  - `a_statement_returns_arrow_batches`: `SELECT _id, n FROM kb ORDER BY n` returns the expected rows, and the result includes tail docs (strong).
  - `the_namespace_comes_from_metadata`: two namespaces, each with a `kb`, give different counts.
  - `a_consistency_token_in_metadata_is_honoured`.
  - `catalogs_schemas_and_tables_are_listed`, including `include_schema`.
  - `ddl_over_flight_is_invalid_argument`.

**Files:** `crates/operon-query/src/{flight_ingest.rs,flight.rs,sql/json_rows.rs,lib.rs}`, `crates/operon-query/tests/flight_ingest.rs`, `crates/operon/src/api/{streams.rs,mod.rs}`, `crates/operon/src/server.rs`, `crates/operon/tests/flight_ingest.rs`.

**Consumes:**
- Task 9: `CollectionService::{write, get_collection, create_collection}`, `WriteOptions`, `WriteResult`, `ServiceError`. Task 10: `COLLECTIONS_SCHEMA`, `collection_arrow_schema`, and the Arrow-to-JSON value conversion of `rows_to_json` (rule 7). Task 12: `OperonFlightSql`, `FlightConfig`, `NAMESPACE_METADATA`, `serve_flight_sql`, the `ServiceError` → `tonic::Status` mapping (rule 5), `register_sql_info`.
- M1.1: `PrimaryKey`, `Document`, `DocOp::Upsert`, `SparseVector::new`, `ConsistencyToken` (`merge`, `Display`), `MAX_WRITE_OPS` (10 000), `CollectionSchema`, `VectorSpec`, `SparseVectorSpec`, `HnswParams::default()`, and `partition_of`'s hash (`xxhash_rust::xxh3::xxh3_64`, `pk.rs:99-102`).
- M0 as built: the produce handler (`crates/operon/src/api.rs` `produce`, `stream_id`), `LogWriter::append_many(stream, Vec<(u32, Vec<Record>)>)` (one WAL object and one `CommitWal` for every partition of the call), `Record { key, value, headers, timestamp_ms }`, `AppendAck { stream, partition, base_offset, last_offset }`. Task 11 rule 4 (the implicit-stream partition check).
- `arrow-flight 58.4` (`flight-sql-experimental`), checked in `arrow-flight-58.4.0/src/`:
  - `sql/server.rs:410` `FlightSqlService::do_put_statement_ingest(&self, CommandStatementIngest, Request<PeekableFlightDataStream>) -> Result<i64, Status>`. The provided `do_put` answers it with one `PutResult` whose `app_metadata` is `DoPutUpdateResult { record_count }` (`:746-753`), so this entry point carries a row count and nothing else.
  - `sql/server.rs:378` `FlightSqlService::do_put_fallback(&self, Request<PeekableFlightDataStream>, Any) -> Result<Response<DoPutStream>, Status>`. The provided `do_put` decodes `FlightDescriptor.cmd` as `Any` and sends every type URL it does not know here (`:711-782`), including the empty `cmd` of a PATH descriptor (type URL `""`). `do_put_error_callback` (`:390`) receives a missing command or descriptor.
